./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g2-effect-state.txt:48:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:191:| Decision | RepositoryOperationStatus 使用 `ACCEPTED/RUNNING/RECONCILING/SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELLED/ATTENTION_REQUIRED`，并与 journal phase、effect classification、cleanup state 和 FailureDescriptor 分字段表达。SUCCEEDED 只对应 VERIFIED；FAILED 只用于已证明 `NONE/NOT_APPLIED` 且自动处理终止；CANCELLED 同样要求 `NONE/NOT_APPLIED`；ATTENTION_REQUIRED 是可继续修复的 operational parking，不证明无 effect，也不释放 writer occupancy。Repository phase 固定为 `ACCEPT/PREPARE/EXECUTE/PUBLISH/VERIFY/RECONCILE/CLEANUP`。接受前 rejection 返回 typed problem response且不创建operation；接受后 failure由GetOperation representation承载，查询已存在operation本身不是HTTP失败；Current同步mutation直接返回typed failure并把确定结果绑定原idempotency key。Repository Service维护versioned stable code与typed details，至少覆盖request invalid、idempotency conflict、repository readiness/not-found、generation/ref/name/protected conflict、artifact/import rejection、dependency/capacity failure、effect unknown、readback integrity、recovery attention和internal invariant；`retryable`如保留只从recovery派生。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/README.md:68:| Failure representation → crash classification | `P2-R6-D01`、`P2-R6-D02`、`P2-R6-D15` | Operation status、journal phase、effect classification和FailureDescriptor分离；普通verification mismatch与authority corruption分开处理。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/README.md:98:- `UNKNOWN/APPLIED` 不得 blind replay 或释放业务/Repository serialization
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:116:| Rejected alternatives | 只保存 PREPARED/APPLIED/VERIFIED：effect 调用窗口崩溃时缺少已开始证据；从 Dolt log/ref 临时推导全部状态：无法恢复 request digest、幂等碰撞、未形成 commit 的尝试或 Current effect；provider 返回成功即 VERIFIED：绕过强制 committed-ref readback。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:189:| Topic | Caller-visible operation status and FailureDescriptor taxonomy |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:191:| Decision | RepositoryOperationStatus 使用 `ACCEPTED/RUNNING/RECONCILING/SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELLED/ATTENTION_REQUIRED`，并与 journal phase、effect classification、cleanup state 和 FailureDescriptor 分字段表达。SUCCEEDED 只对应 VERIFIED；FAILED 只用于已证明 `NONE/NOT_APPLIED` 且自动处理终止；CANCELLED 同样要求 `NONE/NOT_APPLIED`；ATTENTION_REQUIRED 是可继续修复的 operational parking，不证明无 effect，也不释放 writer occupancy。Repository phase 固定为 `ACCEPT/PREPARE/EXECUTE/PUBLISH/VERIFY/RECONCILE/CLEANUP`。接受前 rejection 返回 typed problem response且不创建operation；接受后 failure由GetOperation representation承载，查询已存在operation本身不是HTTP失败；Current同步mutation直接返回typed failure并把确定结果绑定原idempotency key。Repository Service维护versioned stable code与typed details，至少覆盖request invalid、idempotency conflict、repository readiness/not-found、generation/ref/name/protected conflict、artifact/import rejection、dependency/capacity failure、effect unknown、readback integrity、recovery attention和internal invariant；`retryable`如保留只从recovery派生。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:192:| Rationale | Operation调度状态、Repository effect事实和调用方恢复动作不是同一维度。分离后才能在UNKNOWN/APPLIED时阻止legacy式FAILED_FINAL或retryable boolean错误终结effect，并让同步、异步和HTTP transport共享同一typed failure语义。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:194:| Constraints | FailureDescriptor公共category/recovery/effect枚举沿用formal authority；stable code、phase和details由Repository use case owner版本化。认证类code在Round 8细化。Operation terminal status不得被业务侧直接解释为T3业务终局。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:196:| Formal owner | Repository Service API/operation contract；Shared Kernel FailureDescriptor仅拥有公共维度。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:197:| Reopen condition | Formal FailureDescriptor维度或Repository operation lifecycle发生breaking change，并提供跨persisted/wire状态迁移方案。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:251:| Decision | Operation独立维护`NOT_REQUIRED/PENDING/COMPLETE/FAILED_ATTENTION` cleanup state；cleanup failure不得覆盖或降级已证明的effect classification。VERIFIED operation即使非结果性临时资源清理失败仍保持SUCCEEDED，并暴露cleanup attention。UNKNOWN/APPLIED时禁止删除可能用于归因、readback或恢复的artifact、staging ref/commit、request manifest、journal/effect marker；明确NOT_APPLIED或完成VERIFIED后才按资源类别进入安全cleanup。Startup发现stale staging时，只有证明无registry、journal、operation或artifact引用后才可自动清理，否则quarantine。Cleanup failure可触发repository/store admission restriction以保护durability，但不能触发effect replay。Append-only audit evidence至少记录operation/journal transition、effect classification变化、request/artifact/evidence digest、executor/fencing、recovery action/reason、caller/operator identity、cleanup/quarantine结果及sanitized diagnostic refs；禁止记录raw rows、secret、token或内部path。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/interactive-design-session-guide.md:360:- PREPARED/APPLIED/VERIFIED journal；
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:375:| Downstream impact | 约束logging、tracing、FailureDescriptor sanitization、operator evidence、retention、security tests和incident流程。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:446:| Decision | HTTP error 使用 `application/problem+json` 基础字段 `type/title/status/detail/instance`，并扩展 formal `FailureDescriptor.code/category/phase/recovery/effect/details/diagnostic_refs`、`request_id` 与 `wire_schema_version`。`detail` 仅供人阅读；`category/recovery/effect` 是机器处理 authority，HTTP status 不决定 retry 或 effect。新增可选字段和 owner-versioned stable code 可 additive 演进；改变既有 code、公共 enum 或 recovery/effect 含义属于 breaking。未知字段须忽略；未知 code 可按已知公共维度降级；未知 recovery/effect 必须 fail closed 且不得自动重放 mutation。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:448:| Rejected alternatives | 只返回自定义 FailureDescriptor：失去标准 media type/tooling；只返回 status + text：无法表达 UNKNOWN/APPLIED 和 reconcile authority；为所有服务建立单一业务 ErrorCode registry：违反 use-case owner 边界。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:451:| Formal owner | Repository Service wire error contract；Shared Kernel FailureDescriptor；HTTP interface contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:452:| Reopen condition | Formal FailureDescriptor 公共维度改变，或 HTTP transport 被正式替换且不再需要 Problem Details mapping。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:661:| Decision | Application Port/SDK 共享 TableSchema/RepositoryLogicalType、projection/filter/order、framed Arrow/Parquet、ArtifactRef、FailureDescriptor及 discriminated `TableReadBinding = VersionedBinding(CommitId) | CurrentBinding(CurrentTableGenerationId)`；wire route、mutation command、snapshot token、result与recovery保持 store-specific。禁止用一个带 store_kind 的 generic Get/Write endpoint抹平 commit/ref 与 current generation语义。 |
